DIVIDEND DISTRIBUTION POLICY                                                   派付股息政策
The Company seeks to maintain a balance between meeting shareholders’           本公司致力通過可持續的股息政策，在符合股
expectations and prudent capital management with sustainable dividend           東期望與審慎資本管理兩者之間保持平衡。本
policy. The Company’s dividend policy aims to allow shareholders to share       公司的股息政策旨在讓股東得以分享本公司的
the Company’s profits and the Company to retain adequate reserves for           利潤，同時讓本公司預留足夠儲備供日後發展
future growth. In proposing any dividend payout, the Company would              之用。在建議宣派股息時，本公司會考慮多
consider various factors including (i) the financial performance and overall    項因素，包括(i)本集團的財務業績及整體財務

equity of the Group; (iii) the liquidity position and capital requirements      率；(iii)本集團的流動資金狀況及資本需求；(iv)
of the Group; (iv) the current and future operation of the Group; (v)           本集團的當前及未來營運；(v)本集團的業務發
the business development strategy and future expansion plans of the             展策略及未來擴充計劃；(vi)整體市況；(vii)香港
Group; (vi) the general market conditions; (vii) any relevant requirements      聯合交易所有限公司（   「聯交所」）證券上市規則

the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) and              公司細則的任何相關規定；及(viii)董事會認為相
applicable laws, rules and regulations as well as the Company’s Articles of     關的任何其他因素。經過詳細論證後，履行相
Association; and (viii) any other factors which the Board deems relevant.       應的決策程序，末期股息經股東大會批准。在

BOARD MEETINGS                                                               董事會會議

     1. Emissions                                                                   1. 排放物
        Since its founding, the Company has always held on to its                       本公司自成立以來，始終把肩負的環

 working hours are 8 hours a day. According to the Management Rules               每天8小時。依據《考勤休假管理規定》，確
 for Work Attendance and Leave, we ensure that employees can enjoy                保員工享有當地法定假期與休息日。員工

 in advance and get the confirmation and approval of their superior               依據實際加班情況計算加班，若不能安排
 leaders. Overtime pay is calculated on a monthly basis according to              調休或員工本人未提出調休申請的，按勞
 the actual overtime work. If it is impossible to arrange compensatory            動法規計算並支付員工加班費。
 leave or the employee doesn’t apply for compensatory leave, the
 overtime pay is calculated and paid according to the Labor Law.

 There were no work-related death accidents from 2020 to 2022,               二零二零年到二零二二年度未發生工亡事
 and the number of days lost due to work-related injuries in 2022 was        故，二零二二年工傷損失日數為670天（千
 670 (the injury rate per thousand people was 0.15%). Interviews are         人負傷率為0.15%）。對發生一般及以上責
 held with units that are responsible for general or serious accidents,      任性事故單位進行事故約談，事故（問題）
 and accidents (problems) are dealt with by drawing inferences from          處理同時還要舉一反三、觸類旁通，擴大
 other facts, so as to expand the scope of education and rectification.      教育整改面。事故處理要「實、細、嚴」，
 Accidents should be handled in a “factual, detailed and strict” manner.     所謂「實」：要實事求是，以事實為依據，
 “Factual” means to seek truth from facts and to be based on facts           不弄虛作假，不草率從事；「細」：要認真
 rather than practice fraud or act rashly. “Detailed” means to be            細緻，不漏蛛絲馬跡，不放過任何可疑跡
 careful and meticulous without missing any clues or letting go of any       象；「嚴」：對事故責任人要從嚴處理，不
 suspicious signs. “Strict” means to impose strict punishments on those      姑息遷就，不敷衍了事。有些事故在追查
 held responsible for the accidents without appeasement or perfunctory       責任時，還要上下聯繫，左右掛鈎，以進
 treatment. When pursuing liability for some accidents, it is necessary      一步增強相關人員的責任心。
 to sort out the cause-and-effect relationship and take into account
 related factors, so as to further enhance the sense of responsibility of
 the relevant personnel.

    disguised forced labor, in order to actively promote local employment            強迫勞動的行為，積極推動當地就業和社
    and social and economic development.                                             會經濟發展。

    of child labor, and the identity information of new employees will be            入職、面試、錄用這幾個時間節點均會嚴
    strictly checked at the time points of entry, interview and employment,          格查驗其身份信息，從源頭處防止誤錄童
    so as to prevent the employment of child labor from the source. Once             工。對於誤用童工的現象，一經發現，將

    link will be reviewed and rectify in a timely manner to prevent any              現誤用童工的現象。

    Provision for impairment on trade receivables 應收貿易賬款的減值撥備

Management uses a provision matrix to calculate expected credit losses for        管理層使用撥備矩陣計算應收貿易賬款之預期
trade receivables. The application of provision matrix requires to consider       信貸虧損。使用撥備矩陣須考慮所有合理及可
all reasonable and reliable information, including customers’ credit risks,       靠資料，包括客戶的信貸風險、應收賬款賬
ageing of receivables, existence of disputes and historic payments, as            齡、是否存在糾紛及以往付款以及未來經濟狀
well as forecasts of future economic conditions. The groupings of various         況預測。各客戶分部的分類及預期信貸虧損率
customer segments and the estimation of expected credit loss rate involve         的估計涉及重大判斷及估計。
significant judgements and estimates.

    Inventory provision 存貨撥備


As at 31 December 2022, inventories and related provision amounted to          於二零二二年十二月三十一日，存貨及相關
RMB17,217,957,000 and RMB171,551,000, respectively, which were                 撥備分別為人民幣17,217,957,000元及人民幣
material to the consolidated financial statements.                             171,551,000元，對合併財務報表而言屬重大。

Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable value. The      存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較低者列
inventories of the Group are mobile intelligent terminals and other products   賬。 貴集團存貨主要為產品革新及技術升級
which are subject to rapid product innovations and technological upgrades      迅速的移動智能終端及其他產品，故廢棄風險
and therefore have a high risk of obsolescence. Management assessment          極高。管理層對存貨撥備的評估為主觀判斷，
on inventory provision is judgemental and is based on assumptions,             乃基於預測存貨用途、預計售價及完成及出售
specifically the forecasted inventory usage, estimated selling prices and      產生的成本所作之假設，而該等假設受預期未
cost to be incurred to completion and disposal, which are affected by          來市場及銷售訂單所影響。
expected future market and sales orders.

The accounting policies and disclosures for inventory provision are included   存貨撥備之會計政策及披露載於合併財務報表
in notes 2.4, 3 and 18 to the consolidated financial statements.               附註2.4、3及18。

We performed the following procedures, among others, on the inventory          我們就存貨撥備進行（其中包括）以下程序：
provision:

•     Checked management’s methodology and estimates used in the               •   檢查計算存貨撥備時管理層所用的方法及
      inventory provision calculation;                                             估計；

•     Assessed the reasonableness of the inventory provision by comparing      •   與過往撇減、實際售價及銷售成本進行比
      it to historical write-downs, actual selling prices and costs to sale;       較，評估存貨撥備是否合理；

•     Tested the ageing of inventories and discussed the long ageing           •   測試存貨的賬齡及與管理層討論賬齡較長
      inventories with management to identify any slow-moving, excess or           的存貨，以識別出任何滯銷、多餘或廢棄
      obsolete items;                                                              產品；

•     Observed the stocktaking process at the end of the year; and             •   觀察年末資產盤點過程；及

•     Performed subsequent sales review of inventories.                        •   對存貨進行期後銷售審閱。

2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                          2.1 編製基準
  These financial statements have been prepared in accordance with                  此等財務報表乃按照由香港會計師公會
  Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) (which include                （「香 港 會 計 師 公 會」）頒 佈 的《香 港 財 務 報
  all Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards, Hong Kong Accounting                 告準則》 （「《香港財務報告準則》」      ）
                                                                                                            （包括所
  Standards (“HKASs”) and Interpretations) issued by the Hong Kong                  有《香港財務報告準則》、《香港會計準則》
  Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”), accounting                 （「《香港會計準則》」    ）及詮釋）、香港公認會
  principles generally accepted in Hong Kong and the Hong Kong                      計原則及香港公司條例而編製。此等財務
  Companies Ordinance. They have been prepared under the historical                 報表乃按照歷史成本法編製，惟若干指定
  cost convention, except for certain equity investments designated at              為以公允價值計量並計入其他綜合收益的
  fair value through other comprehensive income, financial assets at                權益投資、以公允價值計量並計入損益的
  fair value through profit or loss, receivables financing and derivative           金融資產、應收款項融資及衍生金融工具
  financial instruments, which have been measured at fair value. These              除外，其乃以公允價值計量。此等財務報
  financial statements are presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values             表以人民幣（  「人民幣」   ）呈列，除另有指明
  are rounded to the nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.              外，所有價值乃約整至最接近的千位。


  Basis of consolidation                                                            合併基準
  The consolidated financial statements include the financial statements            合併財務報表包括本集團截至二零二二年
  of the Group for the year ended 31 December 2022. A subsidiary is                 十二月三十一日止年度之財務報表。附屬
  an entity (including a structured entity), directly or indirectly, controlled     公司乃一間由本公司直接或間接控制的實
  by the Company. Control is achieved when the Group is exposed, or                 體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團對參與投
  has rights, to variable returns from its involvement with the investee            資對象業務的浮動回報承擔風險或享有權
  and has the ability to affect those returns through its power over the            利以及能透過對投資對象的權力（即本集團
  investee (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the current ability to        獲賦予現有能力以主導投資對象相關活動
  direct the relevant activities of the investee).                                  的既存權利）影響該等回報時，即取得控制
                                                                                    權。

  Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting rights results        一般情況下，假設多數投票權形成控制
  in control. When the Company has, directly or indirectly, less than               權。倘本公司直接或間接擁有少於投資對
  a majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group              象大多數投票權或類似權利，則本集團於
  considers all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing whether it            評估其是否擁有對投資對象的權力時會考
  has power over an investee, including:                                            慮一切相關事實及情況，包括：

  (a) the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of the                (a) 與投資對象其他投票持有人的合同安
      investee;                                                                         排；

  (b) rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and                       (b) 其他合同安排所產生的權利；及

  (c) the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                        (c) 本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (Continued)                                                 2.1 編製基準（續）
      Basis of consolidation (Continued)                                               合併基準（續）
      The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for the                附屬公司與本公司的財務報表的報告期間
      same reporting period as the Company, using consistent accounting                相同，並採用一致會計政策編製。附屬公

  the former owners of the acquiree and the equity interests issued             取被收購方控制權的股本權益於收購日期
  by the Group in exchange for control of the acquiree. For each                的公允價值總和。於各業務合併中，本集
  business combination, the Group elects whether to measure the                 團選擇是否以公允價值或被收購方可識別
  non-controlling interests in the acquiree that are present ownership          資產淨值的應佔比例，計量於被收購方的
  interests and entitle their holders to a proportionate share of net assets    非控股權益（屬現時擁有人權益並賦予擁有
  in the event of liquidation at fair value or at the proportionate share of    人權利於清盤時按比例分佔其資產淨值）。
  the acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All other components of non-          非控股權益的所有其他組成部分乃以公允
  controlling interests are measured at fair value. Acquisition-related         價值計量。收購相關成本於產生時列為開
  costs are expensed as incurred.                                               支。

      Business combinations (Continued)                                               業務合併（續）
      When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial assets            當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合同條
      and liabilities assumed for appropriate classification and designation          款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相關條件，
      in accordance with the contractual terms, economic circumstances                評估將承擔的金融資產及負債，以作出適
      and pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the          合的分類及指定，其中包括將被收購方主
      separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of the acquiree.           合同中的嵌入式衍生工具進行分離。

      If the business combination is achieved in stages, the previously held          倘業務合併分階段進行，先前持有的股權
      equity interest is remeasured at its acquisition date fair value and any        按收購日期的公允價值重新計量，而任何
      resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.                         所得收益或虧損則於損益中確認。

      Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the acquirer                  收購方所轉讓的任何或然代價按於收購日
      is recognised at fair value at the acquisition date. Contingent                 期的公允價值確認。分類為資產或負債的
      consideration classified as an asset or liability is measured at fair value     或然代價以公允價值計量，公允價值變動
      with changes in fair value recognised in profit or loss. Contingent             確認於損益內。分類為權益的或然代價並
      consideration that is classified as equity is not remeasured and                無重新計量，而其後結算於權益中入賬。
      subsequent settlement is accounted for within equity.


      Fair value measurement                                                          公允價值計量
      The Group measures its listed equity investments and derivative                 本集團於各報告期末以公允價值計量其上
      financial instruments at fair value at the end of each reporting period.        市股本投資及衍生金融工具。公允價值指
      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to      市場參與者於計量日期在有序交易中出售
      transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants      資產所收取或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公
      at the measurement date. The fair value measurement is based on the             允價值計量所依據的假設為，出售資產或
      presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability    轉讓負債的交易乃於該資產或負債的主要
      takes place either in the principal market for the asset or liability, or in    市場進行，或如無主要市場，則於對該資
      the absence of a principal market, in the most advantageous market              產或負債最為有利的市場進行。本集團必
      for the asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market       須可進入該主要或最為有利的市場。計量
      must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability      資產或負債的公允價值使用市場參與者於
      is measured using the assumptions that market participants would use            為該資產或負債定價時所依據的假設，即
      when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market participants          假設市場參與者按其最佳經濟利益行事。
      act in their economic best interest.

      A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account            非金融資產公允價值的計量參考市場參與
      a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using           者可從使用該資產得到的最高及最佳效
      the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market        用，或把該資產售予另一可從使用該資產
      participant that would use the asset in its highest and best use.               得到最高及最佳效用的市場參與者所產生
                                                                                      的經濟效益。

  Investments and other financial assets                                         投資及其他金融資產
  Initial recognition and measurement                                            初步確認及計量
  Financial assets are classified, at initial recognition, as subsequently       金融資產於初步確認時分類為其後以攤餘
  measured at amortised cost, fair value through other comprehensive             成本、以公允價值計量並計入其他綜合收
  income, and fair value through profit or loss.                                 益及以公允價值計量並計入損益計量。

      In order for a financial asset to be classified and measured at                 為使金融資產以攤餘成本或以公允價值計
      amortised cost or fair value through other comprehensive income, it             量並計入其他綜合收益進行分類及計量，
      needs to give rise to cash flows that are solely payments of principal          需產生僅為支付本金及未償還本金利息
      and interest (“SPPI”) on the principal amount outstanding. Financial           （
                                                                                     「SPPI」
                                                                                          ）的現金流量。現金流量並非SPPI
      assets with cash flows that are not SPPI are classified and measured            的金融資產以公允價值計量並計入損益分
      at fair value through profit or loss, irrespective of the business model.       類及計量，不論其業務模式如何。

      The Group’s business model for managing financial assets refers to             本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其管理
      how it manages its financial assets in order to generate cash flows.           其金融資產以產生現金流量的方式。業務
      The business model determines whether cash flows will result from              模式釐定現金流量是否來自收集合同現金
      collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial assets, or both.      流量、出售金融資產，或兩者兼有。以攤
      Financial assets classified and measured at amortised cost are held            餘成本分類及計量的金融資產於旨在持有
      within a business model with the objective to hold financial assets in         金融資產於以收取合同現金流量的業務模
      order to collect contractual cash flows, while financial assets classified     式中持有，而以公允價值分類及計量並計
      and measured at fair value through other comprehensive income are              入其他綜合收益的金融資產於旨在持有以
      held within a business model with the objective of both holding to             收取合同現金流量及銷售的業務模式中持
      collect contractual cash flows and selling. Financial assets which are         有。並非於上述業務模式持有的金融資產
      not held within the aforementioned business models are classified and          以公允價值分類及計量並計入損益。
      measured at fair value through profit or loss.

      All regular way purchases and sales of financial assets are recognised         所有一般買賣的金融資產概於交易日（即本
      on the trade date, that is, the date that the Group commits to purchase        集團承諾買賣該資產的日期）予以確認。一
      or sell the asset. Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or             般買賣乃指按照一般市場規定或慣例須在
      sales of financial assets that require delivery of assets within the period    一定期間內交付資產的金融資產買賣。
      generally established by regulation or convention in the marketplace.

      Subsequent measurement                                                         後續計量

      classification as follows:                                                     定：

      Financial assets at amortised cost (debt instruments)                          以攤餘成本計量的金融資產（債務工具）

      the effective interest method and are subject to impairment. Gains and         利率法計量，並受限於減值。當資產終止
      losses are recognised in the statement of profit or loss when the asset        確認、修訂或減值時，收益及虧損於損益
      is derecognised, modified or impaired.                                         表中確認。

      The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses                      本集團確認對並非持作以公允價值計量並

      that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an approximation                 與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量之間的
      of the original effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will             差額而釐定，並以原實際利率的近似值折
      include cash flows from the sale of collateral held or other credit               現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押的
      enhancements that are integral to the contractual terms.                          現金流量或組成合同條款的其他信貸提升
                                                                                        措施的現金流量。

  For trade receivables that do not contain a significant financing                 就不包含顯著融資組成部分或倘本集團應
  component or when the Group applies the practical expedient of                    用不調整顯著融資組成部分影響的實際權
  not adjusting the effect of a significant financing component, the                宜方法的應收貿易賬款，本集團應用簡化
  Group applies the simplified approach in calculating ECLs. Under the              法計算預期信貸虧損。根據簡化法，本集
  simplified approach, the Group does not track changes in credit risk,             團並無追蹤信貸風險的變動，反而於各報
  but instead recognises a loss allowance based on lifetime ECLs at                 告日期根據年限內預期信貸虧損確認虧
  each reporting date. The Group has established a provision matrix that            損撥備。本集團已根據其以往信貸虧損經
  is based on its historical credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-          驗，建立撥備矩陣，並就債務人及經濟環
  looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic environment.             境的特定前瞻性因素作出調整。


  Financial liabilities                                                             金融負債
  Initial recognition and measurement                                               初步確認及計量
  Financial liabilities issued by a group entity are classified as either           集團實體發行的金融負債根據合同安排的
  financial liabilities or as equity in accordance with the substance of the        內容以及金融負債及股本工具的釋義分類
  contractual arrangements and the definitions of a financial liability and         為金融負債或股本工具。
  an equity instrument.

  All financial liabilities are recognised initially at fair value and, in the      所有金融負債於初步以公允價值確認，而
  case of loans and borrowings and payable, net of directly attributable            貸款及借貸及應付賬款則於扣除直接應佔
  transaction costs.                                                                交易成本後確認。

      Financial liabilities at amortised cost (loans and borrowings)                   以攤餘成本計量的金融負債（貸款及借貸）

      subsequently measured at amortised cost, using the effective interest            際利率法以攤餘成本計量，惟折現影響甚

      which case they are stated at cost. Gains and losses are recognised in           於負債終止確認時採用實際利率攤銷程序
      the statement of profit or loss when the liabilities are derecognised as         於損益表內予以確認。
      well as through the effective interest rate amortisation process.

      Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account any discount or              計量攤餘成本時，計及收購的任何折現或

      included in finance costs in the statement of profit or loss.                    本。


      Derecognition of financial liabilities                                           終止確認金融負債
      A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under the              金融負債於負債的責任已解除或註銷或屆
      liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                                滿時終止確認。

      When an existing financial liability is replaced by another from the             當現有金融負債為同一貸款人以實質上不
      same lender on substantially different terms, or the terms of an existing        同條款的負債取代時，或現有負債的條款
      liability are substantially modified, such an exchange or modification           有重大修改時，有關交換或修改則被視為
      is treated as a derecognition of the original liability and a recognition        終止確認原有負債及確認一項新負債，而
      of a new liability, and the difference between the respective carrying           有關賬面值的差額乃於損益表確認。
      amounts is recognised in the statement of profit or loss.

      constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is probable     定），且很可能須以日後資源流出清償此等

      amount of the obligation.

      provision is the present value at the end of the reporting period of the    於報告期末預期需要清償責任的未來開支

      The increase in the discounted present value amount arising from the        損益表中的融資成本內。
      passage of time is included in finance costs in the statement of profit
      or loss.

  amount of the financial asset.

  terms.                                                                         認。

  Deferred tax assets are recognised for unused tax losses to the extent         僅在可能取得未來應課稅溢利作扣減虧損
  that it is probable that taxable profit will be available against which        的情況下，方確認由未動用稅項虧損產生
  the losses can be utilised. Significant management judgement is                之遞延稅項資產。在釐定可予確認之遞延
  required to determine the amount of deferred tax assets that can be            稅項資產的金額時，須根據可能的時間、
  recognised, based upon the likely timing, and level of future taxable          未來應課稅溢利水平連同未來稅項計劃策
  profits together with future tax planning strategies. Further details are      略作出重大管理層判斷。進一步詳情載於
  included in note 27 to the financial statements.                               財務報表附註27。

   ESTIMATES (Continued)
      Estimation uncertainty (Continued)                                            估計不確定因素（續）
      Write-down of inventories based on the lower of cost and net realisable       根據成本及可變現淨值兩者中之較低者撇
      value                                                                         銷存貨
      The Group, pursuant to the accounting policy for inventories, writes          根據存貨會計政策，本集團自成本中將存
      down inventories from cost to net realisable value and makes provision        貨撇銷至可變現淨值，並利用成本及可變
      for slow-moving items and obsolescence by using the lower of cost             現淨值兩者中較低者對滯銷及廢棄產品作
      and net realisable value rule. The Group makes estimates of the               出撥備。本集團基於過往經驗及現行市
      selling prices, the costs of completion, and the costs to be incurred in      況，估計售價、完成成本及出售產生的成
      disposal based on the historical experience and the prevailing market         本。
      conditions.

      Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing rate                            租賃 – 估算增量借款利率
      The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in a lease,     本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的利
      and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate (“IBR”) to measure       率，因此，使用增量借款利率（ 「增量借款
      lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that the Group would       利率」 ）計量租賃負債。增量借款利率為本
      have to pay to borrow over a similar term, and with a similar security,       集團於類似經濟環境中為取得與使用權
      the funds necessary to obtain an asset of a similar value to the right-of-    資產價值相近之資產，而以類似抵押品於
      use asset in a similar economic environment. The IBR therefore reflects       類似期間借入所需資金應支付之利率。因
      what the Group “would have to pay”, which requires estimation when            此，增量借款利率反映了本集團「應支付」
      no observable rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that do not       的利率，當無可觀察的利率時（如就並無訂
      enter into financing transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted to         立融資交易之附屬公司而言）或當須對利率
      reflect the terms and conditions of the lease (for example, when leases       進行調整以反映租賃之條款及條件時（如當
      are not in the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group estimates         租賃並非以附屬公司的功能貨幣計價時），
      the IBR using observable inputs (such as market interest rates) when          則須作出估計。當可觀察輸入數據可用
      available and is required to make certain entity-specific estimates (such     時，本集團使用可觀察輸入數據（如市場利
      as the subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).                               率）估算增量借款利率，並須針對不同實體
                                                                                    作出若干估計（例如附屬公司的單獨信貸評
                                                                                    級）。

      Capital management                                                                               資本管理
      The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to                                  本集團資本管理的主要目標為確保本集團
      safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern and to                              持續發展的能力及保持良好的資本比率，
      maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its business and                             以支持本集團的業務及盡量提升股東價
      maximise shareholders’ value.                                                                    值。

      The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments to                                 本集團根據經濟狀況的變化管理其資本架
      it in light of changes in economic conditions. To maintain or adjust                             構及作出調整。為保持或調整資本架構，
      the capital structure, the Group may adjust the dividend payment                                 本集團或會調整派付予股東的股息、股東
      to shareholders, return capital to shareholders or issue new shares.                             的資本回報或發行新股。截至二零二二年
      No changes were made in the objectives, policies or processes for                                十二月三十一日及二零二一年十二月三十
      managing capital during the years ended 31 December 2022 and 31                                  一日止年度，並無對管理資本的目標、政
      December 2021.                                                                                   策或程序作出更改。

      The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is net debt                              本集團以資本負債比率（即債務淨值除以權
      divided by equity. The Group’s policy is to maintain the gearing ratio                           益）監察其資本。本集團的政策為將資本負
      as low as possible.                                                                              債比率盡量保持最低。

38. TRANSFERS OF FINANCIAL ASSETS                                            38. 金融資產轉移
  Financial assets that are derecognised in their entirety                     完全撤銷確認的金融資產
  In the ordinary course of business, the Group has factored a part            本集團在日常業務中因短期融資需求將部
  of receivables to financial institutions without recourse for its short-     分應收賬款以無追溯權方式保理給金融機
  term financing needs, and has entered into non-recourse receivables          構，並與多家銀行訂立無追溯權的應收
  factoring agreements with a number of banks to transfer certain              賬款保理協議，將若干應收賬款轉讓予
  receivables to those banks (the “Receivable Factoring”). Under               該等銀行（   「應收賬款保理」     ）。在若干應收
  certain receivable factoring agreements, the Group is not required           賬款保理協議下，本集團不需要承擔應收
  to undertake default risks and the delayed repayment risk from the           賬款轉讓後的債務人違約風險和延遲還款
  debtors after the transfer of receivables, and all risks and rewards         風險，已轉移應收賬款所有權之所有的風
  related to the ownership of the receivables are transferred. The             險和報酬，符合終止金融資產條件。因
  definition of termination of financial assets is met. Therefore, the         此，本集團對該保理協議下的應收賬款按
  Group derecognised the receivables under the factoring agreements            照賬面值撤銷確認。於二零二二年十二
  at carrying amount. As at 31 December 2022, the carrying                     月三十一日，相關保理協議下已轉讓的
